It excludes completed/awarded receipts, invalid state/cycle chronology, live or malformed claim leases, and either terminal history marker. Expired permits remain discoverable when their terminal victory and original cycle chronology are valid; no reward amounts or progression values are returned.
- **Client recovery:** `src/app/api.ts` bounds the discovery request, validates the response, and deduplicates permit IDs. `src/app/AppController.ts` continues to retry locally remembered permits, then discovers server-side pending victories so recovery no longer depends solely on localStorage. Transient lookup failures and temporary `404`/deployment-version mismatches retry after 15 seconds while the same player remains active.
- **Tests changed:** `tests/worker-d1-reward-recovery.mjs` covers conflicting victory and defeat markers, corrupted awarded-receipt replay, and the real local Worker/D1 pending-reward discovery route (including legacy terminal Mana and stale claim recovery). `tests/client-reward-recovery.test.ts` covers discovery after localStorage failure and retry when the new Worker route is not yet deployed. `tests/battle-start-client.test.ts` validates response shape, player-ID URL encoding, and deduplication.
- **QA:** earlier deterministic failures on heads `792dc0b0`, `e49d4fb7`, and `481ffc25` were traced to a cross-VM array assertion and a TypeScript parameter-property fixture unsupported by Node's strip-only loader; both were corrected. Current code/test head's deterministic suite passed in [40828974824](https://github.com/milesgoeswildtv/DEGEN/actions/runs/40828974824). Do not claim full CI until the D1 reward concurrency/recovery, Underpass cycle/rollover, atomic defeat history, production build, Chromium mobile battle vertical slice, and `npm run check:worker` stages pass on the final head.

Degen-only `maxMana` / bespoke `manaCost`, persisted authoritative `player_mana`, reject-without-mutation, exact deduction, zero-cost-at-zero, killing-blow/defeat behavior, cost-aware controls, no monster Mana, no Mana-triggered reversion, and tracked `0006_backfill_active_battle_mana.sql` remain intact. - **Battle authority / reward idempotency:** Worker/D1 remains authoritative for permit state, turn compare-and-swap, terminal outcome, audited receipts, XP/currency/inventory/history, and exactly-once settlement/recovery. The discovery endpoint returns permit identifiers only and cannot grant or calculate rewards.
